ProvenHashes.UMASH.oh_code_sum (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey) (b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block)
  (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode (ProvenHashes.UMASH.oh k b seed) =
    ∑ i,
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode
        (if ↑i + 1 < b.chunks.length then ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k ↑i) b.chunks[i]

ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh_toNat (key data : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) (tag : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) :
  (BitVec.toNat (ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh key data tag).1, BitVec.toNat (ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh key data tag).2) =
    have A := BitVec.toNat (data.1 + key.1);
    have B := BitVec.toNat (data.2 + key.2);
    (A * B % ProvenHashes.UMASH.q,
      (A * B / ProvenHashes.UMASH.q + BitVec.toNat tag) % ProvenHashes.UMASH.q ^^^ A * B % ProvenHashes.UMASH.q)
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh_toNat' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

      (BitVec.toNat tag) (BitVec.toNat M)

ProvenHashes.UMASH.fingerprint_multiplier_slice (L : ℕ) (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey) (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word)
  (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Message) (hx : List.length x ≤ 8 * L) (hy : List.length y ≤ 8 * L) :
  (ProvenHashes.uniformProb fun f =>
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.hashWith k (↑↑f.1) seed x = ProvenHashes.UMASH.hashWith k (↑↑f.1) seed y ∧
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.hashWith k (↑↑f.2) seed x true = ProvenHashes.UMASH.hashWith k (↑↑f.2) seed y true) ≤
    (if ProvenHashes.UMASH.modePolynomial false k seed x = ProvenHashes.UMASH.modePolynomial false k seed y then 1
      else ProvenHashes.UMASH.rootRate L) *
      if ProvenHashes.UMASH.modePolynomial true k seed x = ProvenHashes.UMASH.modePolynomial true k seed y then 1
      else ProvenHashes.UMASH.rootRate L
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.fingerprint_multiplier_slice' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh_signed_class_fibre_unique (w r A A' B₁ B₂ B₁' B₂' tag tag' M m₁ t₁ m₂ t₂ : ℕ) (Q d e : ℤ)
  (hr : 2 ≤ r) (hRQ : 2 ^ r * Q = 2 ^ w) (hd : d % 2 = 1) (hA : ↑A' = ↑A + 2 ^ r * d) (hB₁ : ↑B₁' = ↑B₁ + 2 ^ r * e)
  (hB₂ : ↑B₂' = ↑B₂ + 2 ^ r * e) (h₁ : B₁ < 2 ^ w) (h₂ : B₂ < 2 ^ w) (hm₁ : m₁ < 2 ^ w) (hsub₁ : t₁ &&& m₁ = t₁)
  (hsub₂ : t₂ &&& m₂ = t₂) (hlabelM : m₁ % 2 ^ (r - 1) = m₂ % 2 ^ (r - 1))
  (hlabelC : ↑m₁ - 2 * ↑t₁ ≡ ↑m₂ - 2 * ↑t₂ [ZMOD 2 ^ r]) (hl₁ : A * B₁ % 2 ^ w = A' * B₁' % 2 ^ w)
  (hl₂ : A * B₂ % 2 ^ w = A' * B₂' % 2 ^ w)
  (hx₁ : ProvenHashes.UMASH.enhHighNat (2 ^ w) A B₁ tag M ^^^ ProvenHashes.UMASH.enhHighNat (2 ^ w) A' B₁' tag' M = m₁)
  (hx₂ : ProvenHashes.UMASH.enhHighNat (2 ^ w) A B₂ tag M ^^^ ProvenHashes.UMASH.enhHighNat (2 ^ w) A' B₂' tag' M = m₂)
  (ht₁ : ProvenHashes.UMASH.enhHighNat (2 ^ w) A B₁ tag M &&& m₁ = t₁)
  (ht₂ : ProvenHashes.UMASH.enhHighNat (2 ^ w) A B₂ tag M &&& m₂ = t₂) : B₁ = B₂

ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh_wrap_class_fibre_unique (w r A δ ε B₁ B₂ tag tag' M m₁ t₁ m₂ t₂ : ℕ) (hr2 : 2 ≤ r) (hr : r < w)
  (hδ : 2 ^ r ∣ δ) (hε : 2 ^ r ∣ ε) (hodd : δ / 2 ^ r % 2 = 1) (h₁ : B₁ < 2 ^ w) (h₂ : B₂ < 2 ^ w) (hm₁ : m₁ < 2 ^ w)
  (hsub₁ : t₁ &&& m₁ = t₁) (hsub₂ : t₂ &&& m₂ = t₂) (hlabelM : m₁ % 2 ^ (r - 1) = m₂ % 2 ^ (r - 1))
  (hlabelC : ↑m₁ - 2 * ↑t₁ ≡ ↑m₂ - 2 * ↑t₂ [ZMOD 2 ^ r]) (hwrap : (B₁ + ε) / 2 ^ w = (B₂ + ε) / 2 ^ w)
  (hl₁ : A * B₁ % 2 ^ w = (A + δ) % 2 ^ w * ((B₁ + ε) % 2 ^ w) % 2 ^ w)
  (hl₂ : A * B₂ % 2 ^ w = (A + δ) % 2 ^ w * ((B₂ + ε) % 2 ^ w) % 2 ^ w)
  (hx₁ :
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.enhHighNat (2 ^ w) A B₁ tag M ^^^
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.enhHighNat (2 ^ w) ((A + δ) % 2 ^ w) ((B₁ + ε) % 2 ^ w) tag' M =
      m₁)

        ProvenHashes.UMASH.enhHighNat (2 ^ w) ((A + δ) % 2 ^ w) ((B₂ + ε) % 2 ^ w) tag' M =
      m₂)
  (ht₁ : ProvenHashes.UMASH.enhHighNat (2 ^ w) A B₁ tag M &&& m₁ = t₁)

ProvenHashes.UMASH.mode_same_count_witness (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Message)
  (hx : 8 < List.length x) (hy : 8 < List.length y) (hne : x ≠ y)
  (hc : (ProvenHashes.UMASH.encode x).length = (ProvenHashes.UMASH.encode y).length) :
  ∃ bx byy,
    bx.Valid ∧
      byy.Valid ∧
        (bx.chunks ≠ byy.chunks ∨ ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockTag seed bx ≠ ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockTag seed byy) ∧
          ∀ (mode : Bool) (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey),
            ProvenHashes.UMASH.modePolynomial mode k seed x = ProvenHashes.UMASH.modePolynomial mode k seed y →
              ProvenHashes.UMASH.project (ProvenHashes.UMASH.modeBlock mode k bx seed) =
                ProvenHashes.UMASH.project (ProvenHashes.UMASH.modeBlock mode k byy seed)

  (ProvenHashes.uniformProb fun ab => ProvenHashes.UMASH.highXorEventNat n δ 0 tag tag' e (↑ab.1, ↑ab.2)) ≤
    ↑(2 * v + 1) / 2 ^ n

ProvenHashes.UMASH.single_ph_raw_algebra (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey) (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word)
  (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (hc : ProvenHashes.UMASH.sameCount x y) (i : ℕ) (hi : i + 1 < x.chunks.length)
  (ho : ∀ (j : ℕ), j + 1 < x.chunks.length → j ≠ i → x.chunks.getD j (0, 0) = y.chunks.getD j (0, 0)) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.rawPrimaryMask k seed x y =
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockPHDelta k x y i)
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockENHDelta k seed x y) ∧
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.rawSecondaryMask k seed x y =
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleLane (x.chunks.length - (i + 1)) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockPHDelta k x y i).1,
          ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleLane (x.chunks.length - (i + 1)) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockPHDelta k x y i).2)
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockENHDelta k seed x y)

ProvenHashes.UMASH.mem_maskPatterns_iff (d t : ℕ) :
  t ∈ ProvenHashes.UMASH.maskPatterns d ↔ t &&& d = t ∧ ∃ j < 17, 2 * ↑t = ↑d + (↑j - 8) * ↑ProvenHashes.UMASH.p
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.mem_maskPatterns_iff' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

ProvenHashes.UMASH.cast_polyStep (f acc : ℕ) (x : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) :
  ↑(ProvenHashes.UMASH.polyStep f acc x) =
    ↑f * (↑f * (↑acc + (ProvenHashes.UMASH.project x).1) + (ProvenHashes.UMASH.project x).2)
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.cast_polyStep' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.cast_polyReduce (f : ℕ) (xs : List ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) (acc : ℕ) :
  ↑(ProvenHashes.UMASH.polyReduce f xs acc) =
    List.foldl (fun a x => ↑f * (↑f * (a + (ProvenHashes.UMASH.project x).1) + (ProvenHashes.UMASH.project x).2)) (↑acc)
      xs
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.cast_polyReduce' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.fold_chunk_coefficients (f acc : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Field) (xs : List ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) :
  List.foldl (fun a v => (a + v) * f) acc
      (List.flatMap (fun x => [(ProvenHashes.UMASH.project x).1, (ProvenHashes.UMASH.project x).2]) xs) =
    List.foldl (fun a x => f * (f * (a + (ProvenHashes.UMASH.project x).1) + (ProvenHashes.UMASH.project x).2)) acc xs
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.fold_chunk_coefficients' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

        ProvenHashes.UMASH.hashWith k.1 (↑↑k.2) seed x = ProvenHashes.UMASH.hashWith k.1 (↑↑k.2) seed y) ≤

ProvenHashes.UMASH.long_primary_message_cases (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Message)
  (hx : 8 < List.length x) (hy : 8 < List.length y) (hne : x ≠ y)
  (hc : (ProvenHashes.UMASH.encode x).length = (ProvenHashes.UMASH.encode y).length) :
  (∃ bx byy,
      bx.Valid ∧
        byy.Valid ∧
          bx.byteSize = 256 ∧
            byy.byteSize = 256 ∧
              bx.chunks ≠ byy.chunks ∧
                ∀ (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey),
                  ProvenHashes.UMASH.comparisonPolynomial k seed x = ProvenHashes.UMASH.comparisonPolynomial k seed y →
                    ProvenHashes.UMASH.primaryEvent seed bx byy k) ∨
    ∃ bs bx byy,
      bx.Valid ∧
        byy.Valid ∧
          (bx.chunks ≠ byy.chunks ∨ ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockTag seed bx ≠ ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockTag seed byy) ∧
            ProvenHashes.UMASH.encode x = bs ++ [bx] ∧ ProvenHashes.UMASH.encode y = bs ++ [byy]

ProvenHashes.UMASH.secondaryBody_code_sum (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey) (b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block)
  (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode (ProvenHashes.UMASH.secondaryBody k b seed) =
    ∑ i,
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.shuffle
          (if ↑i + 1 < b.chunks.length then ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k ↑i) b.chunks[i]
          else
            ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k ↑i) b.chunks[i] (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockTag seed b))
          (↑i + 1) b.chunks.length)
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.secondaryBody_code_sum' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.secondaryBody_update_code_constant (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey) (b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block)
  (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) (hlen : b.chunks.length ≤ 16) (i : Fin b.chunks.length)
  (hph : ↑i + 1 < b.chunks.length) (s : Fin 2) :
  ∃ C,
    ∀ (v : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word),
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.secondaryBody (Function.update k (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phKeyIndex (↑i) s) v) b seed) =
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.shuffle
              (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph
                (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair (Function.update k (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phKeyIndex (↑i) s) v) ↑i)
                b.chunks[i])
              (↑i + 1) b.chunks.length) +
          C

ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleWide_reference_difference (a b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) (i n : ℕ) (hi : i < n) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.split
      (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleWide (n - i) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.join (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk a b))) =
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.shuffle a i n) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.shuffle b i n)

ProvenHashes.UMASH.shortHash_secondary_eq_mixer (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey) (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word)
  (m : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Message) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.shortHash k seed m true =
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.shortFinish
      (ProvenHashes.UMASH.shortPrepare (ProvenHashes.UMASH.shortPack m) ^^^
        seed + ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyWord k (List.length m + 4))

ProvenHashes.UMASH.phDiffCount_one_index (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (hc : ProvenHashes.UMASH.sameCount x y)
  (hp : ProvenHashes.UMASH.phDiffCount x y = 1) :
  ∃ i,
    i + 1 < x.chunks.length ∧
      x.chunks.getD i (0, 0) ≠ y.chunks.getD i (0, 0) ∧
        ∀ (j : ℕ), j + 1 < x.chunks.length → j ≠ i → x.chunks.getD j (0, 0) = y.chunks.getD j (0, 0)

ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode_mapIdx_sum (f : ℕ → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk)
  (xs : List ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode (List.foldl ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (0, 0) (List.mapIdx f xs)) =
    ∑ j, ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode (f (↑j) (xs.getD ↑j (0, 0)))

ProvenHashes.UMASH.mapped_xor_single (f : ℕ → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk)
  (xs ys : List ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) (hc : xs.length = ys.length) (i : ℕ) (hi : i < xs.length)
  (ho : ∀ j < xs.length, j ≠ i → xs.getD j (0, 0) = ys.getD j (0, 0)) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (List.foldl ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (0, 0) (List.mapIdx f xs))
      (List.foldl ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (0, 0) (List.mapIdx f ys)) =
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (f i (xs.getD i (0, 0))) (f i (ys.getD i (0, 0)))

ProvenHashes.UMASH.nonfinal_mapped_xor_single (f : ℕ → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk)
  (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (hc : ProvenHashes.UMASH.sameCount x y) (i : ℕ) (hi : i + 1 < x.chunks.length)
  (ho : ∀ (j : ℕ), j + 1 < x.chunks.length → j ≠ i → x.chunks.getD j (0, 0) = y.chunks.getD j (0, 0)) :

      (List.foldl ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (0, 0) (List.mapIdx f y.chunks.dropLast)) =
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (f i (x.chunks.getD i (0, 0))) (f i (y.chunks.getD i (0, 0)))

ProvenHashes.UMASH.single_ph_primary_difference (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey) (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word)
  (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (hc : ProvenHashes.UMASH.sameCount x y) (i : ℕ) (hi : i + 1 < x.chunks.length)
  (ho : ∀ (j : ℕ), j + 1 < x.chunks.length → j ≠ i → x.chunks.getD j (0, 0) = y.chunks.getD j (0, 0)) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.oh k x seed) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.oh k y seed) =
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
      (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k i) (x.chunks.getD i (0, 0)))
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k i) (y.chunks.getD i (0, 0))))
      (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k (x.chunks.length - 1)) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.lastChunk x)
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockTag seed x))
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k (x.chunks.length - 1)) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.lastChunk y)
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockTag seed y)))
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.single_ph_primary_difference' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.shuffle_nonfinal_xor (a b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) (i n : ℕ) (hi : i < n) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.shuffle a i n) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.shuffle b i n) =
    (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleLane (n - i) (a.1 ^^^ b.1), ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleLane (n - i) (a.2 ^^^ b.2))

ProvenHashes.UMASH.single_ph_secondary_difference (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey) (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word)
  (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (hc : ProvenHashes.UMASH.sameCount x y) (i : ℕ) (hi : i + 1 < x.chunks.length)
  (ho : ∀ (j : ℕ), j + 1 < x.chunks.length → j ≠ i → x.chunks.getD j (0, 0) = y.chunks.getD j (0, 0)) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.secondaryBody k x seed) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.secondaryBody k y seed) =
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
      (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleLane (x.chunks.length - (i + 1))
          ((ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k i) (x.chunks.getD i (0, 0))).1 ^^^
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k i) (y.chunks.getD i (0, 0))).1),
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleLane (x.chunks.length - (i + 1))
          ((ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k i) (x.chunks.getD i (0, 0))).2 ^^^
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k i) (y.chunks.getD i (0, 0))).2))
      (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k (x.chunks.length - 1)) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.lastChunk x)
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockTag seed x))
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k (x.chunks.length - 1)) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.lastChunk y)
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockTag seed y)))

  (hc : ProvenHashes.UMASH.sameCount x y) (hs : ProvenHashes.UMASH.dataChecksum x = ProvenHashes.UMASH.dataChecksum y)

ProvenHashes.UMASH.tag_product_reconstruct (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) :
  BitVec.toNat k.1 * BitVec.toNat k.2 =
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.q * BitVec.toNat (ProvenHashes.UMASH.tagProductHigh k) +

  [Nonempty V] [DecidableEq I] (E : (I → V) → Prop) (i j k : I) (hij : i ≠ j) (hik : i ≠ k) (hjk : j ≠ k) (bound : ℚ≥0)

    ∀ (K : I → V),
      (ProvenHashes.uniformProb fun abc =>

ProvenHashes.UMASH.probability_partition_prod_le.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {A : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2} {T : Type u_3}
  [Fintype A] [Fintype B] (E : A × B → Prop) (F : T → A → Prop) (targets : Finset T) (count weight : T → ℚ≥0)
  (hcover : ∀ (a : A) (b : B), E (a, b) → ∃ t ∈ targets, F t a)
  (hslice : ∀ t ∈ targets, ∀ (a : A), F t a → (ProvenHashes.uniformProb fun b => E (a, b)) ≤ weight t)
  (hcount : ∀ t ∈ targets, ProvenHashes.uniformProb (F t) ≤ count t) :
  ProvenHashes.uniformProb E ≤ ∑ t ∈ targets, count t * weight t

ProvenHashes.UMASH.two_ph_affine_atom (x y x' y' C D u v : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) (hxy : x ≠ y) (hxy' : x' ≠ y')
  (s t : ℕ) (ht : 0 < t) (hst : t < s) (hs : s ≤ 64) :
  (ProvenHashes.uniformProb fun ab =>
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
              (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph ab.1 x) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph ab.1 y))
              (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph ab.2 x') (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph ab.2 y')))
            C =
          u ∧
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
              (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleChunk s
                (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph ab.1 x) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph ab.1 y)))
              (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleChunk t
                (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph ab.2 x') (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph ab.2 y'))))
            D =
          v) ≤
    2 ^ ProvenHashes.UMASH.phDifferenceShift s t / ↑ProvenHashes.UMASH.q ^ 2
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.two_ph_affine_atom' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.two_ph_affine_compatibility (s t : ℕ) (ht : 0 < t) (hst : t < s)
  (hh : 3 ≤ ProvenHashes.UMASH.phDifferenceShift s t) (a b C D : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) :
  have U := ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk a b) C;
  have V :=
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
      (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleChunk s a) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleChunk t b)) D;
  (BitVec.toNat V.1 ^^^ 2 * BitVec.toNat U.1) % 8 = BitVec.toNat (D.1 ^^^ C.1 <<< 1) % 8 ∧
    (BitVec.toNat V.2 ^^^ 2 * BitVec.toNat U.2) % 8 = BitVec.toNat (D.2 ^^^ C.2 <<< 1) % 8
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.two_ph_affine_compatibility' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph_right_difference_affine (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) (fixed k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (fixed, k) x) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (fixed, k) y) =
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.split (ProvenHashes.UMASH.clmul (x.1 ^^^ y.1) k))
      (ProvenHashes.UMASH.split
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.clmul (x.1 ^^^ fixed) x.2 ^^^ ProvenHashes.UMASH.clmul (y.1 ^^^ fixed) y.2))
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph_right_difference_affine' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode_code_sum (secondary : Bool) (k : ProvenHashes.UMASH.OHKey) (b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block)
  (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode (ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode secondary k b seed) =
    ∑ i,
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary b.chunks.length (↑i)
          (if ↑i + 1 < b.chunks.length then ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k ↑i) b.chunks[i]
          else
            ProvenHashes.UMASH.enh (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPair k ↑i) b.chunks[i] (ProvenHashes.UMASH.blockTag seed b)))
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode_code_sum' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode_pair_code_constant (secondary : Bool) (K : Fin 17 → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk)
  (b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) (hlen : b.chunks.length ≤ 16)
  (i : Fin b.chunks.length) (hph : ↑i + 1 < b.chunks.length) :
  ∃ C,
    ∀ (a : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk),
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode secondary (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPairsEquiv.symm (Function.update K ⟨↑i, ⋯⟩ a)) b
            seed) =
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.chunkCode
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary b.chunks.length (↑i) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph a b.chunks[i])) +
          C
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode_pair_code_constant' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode_pair_update (secondary : Bool) (K : Fin 17 → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk)
  (b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) (hlen : b.chunks.length ≤ 16)
  (i : Fin b.chunks.length) (hph : ↑i + 1 < b.chunks.length) (a : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode secondary (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPairsEquiv.symm (Function.update K ⟨↑i, ⋯⟩ a)) b seed =
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode secondary (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPairsEquiv.symm K) b seed)
      (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary b.chunks.length (↑i) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph (K ⟨↑i, ⋯⟩) b.chunks[i]))
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary b.chunks.length (↑i) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph a b.chunks[i])))

ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode_two_pair_mask (secondary : Bool) (K : Fin 17 → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk)
  (b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) (hlen : b.chunks.length ≤ 16)
  (i j : Fin b.chunks.length) (hij : i ≠ j) (hi : ↑i + 1 < b.chunks.length) (hj : ↑j + 1 < b.chunks.length) :
  ∃ M,
    ∀ (a d : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk),
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode secondary
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPairsEquiv.symm (Function.update (Function.update K ⟨↑i, ⋯⟩ a) ⟨↑j, ⋯⟩ d)) b seed =
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary b.chunks.length (↑i) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph a b.chunks[i]))
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary b.chunks.length (↑j) (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph d b.chunks[j])))
          M
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode_two_pair_mask' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode_phShuffle (n i : ℕ) (hi : i + 1 < n) (v : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode true n i v = ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleChunk (n - (i + 1)) v
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode_phShuffle' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode_xor (secondary : Bool) (n i : ℕ) (hi : i + 1 < n) (a b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary n i (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk a b) =
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary n i a)
      (ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary n i b)
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode_xor' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode_two_difference (secondary : Bool) (K : Fin 17 → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk)
  (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (hx : x.chunks.length ≤ 16)
  (hc : ProvenHashes.UMASH.sameCount x y) (i j : ℕ) (hij : i < j) (hj : j + 1 < x.chunks.length) :
  ∃ C,
    ∀ (a b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk),
      have K' := Function.update (Function.update K ⟨i, ⋯⟩ a) ⟨j, ⋯⟩ b;
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode secondary (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPairsEquiv.symm K') x seed)
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode secondary (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPairsEquiv.symm K') y seed) =
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary x.chunks.length i
              (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph a (x.chunks.getD i (0, 0)))
                (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph a (y.chunks.getD i (0, 0)))))
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.mixedMode secondary x.chunks.length j
              (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph b (x.chunks.getD j (0, 0)))
                (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph b (y.chunks.getD j (0, 0))))))
          C
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.bodyMode_two_difference' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.two_ph_block_raw_masks (K : Fin 17 → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word)
  (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (hx : x.chunks.length ≤ 16) (hc : ProvenHashes.UMASH.sameCount x y) (i j : ℕ)
  (hij : i < j) (hj : j + 1 < x.chunks.length) :
  ∃ C D,
    ∀ (a b : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk),
      have K' := Function.update (Function.update K ⟨i, ⋯⟩ a) ⟨j, ⋯⟩ b;
      have A :=
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph a (x.chunks.getD i (0, 0)))
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph a (y.chunks.getD i (0, 0)));
      have B :=
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph b (x.chunks.getD j (0, 0)))
          (ProvenHashes.UMASH.ph b (y.chunks.getD j (0, 0)));
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.rawPrimaryMask (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPairsEquiv.symm K') seed x y =
          ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk A B) C ∧
        ProvenHashes.UMASH.rawSecondaryMask (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPairsEquiv.symm K') seed x y =
          ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk
            (ProvenHashes.UMASH.xorChunk (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleChunk (x.chunks.length - (i + 1)) A)
              (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleChunk (x.chunks.length - (j + 1)) B))
            D
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.two_ph_block_raw_masks' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.two_ph_block_slice_bound (K : Fin 17 → ProvenHashes.UMASH.Chunk) (seed : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word)
  (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Block) (hx : x.chunks.length ≤ 16) (hc : ProvenHashes.UMASH.sameCount x y)
  (hsum : ProvenHashes.UMASH.dataChecksum x = ProvenHashes.UMASH.dataChecksum y) (i j : ℕ) (hij : i < j)
  (hj : j + 1 < x.chunks.length) (hdi : x.chunks.getD i (0, 0) ≠ y.chunks.getD i (0, 0))
  (hdj : x.chunks.getD j (0, 0) ≠ y.chunks.getD j (0, 0)) :
  (ProvenHashes.uniformProb fun abw =>
      ProvenHashes.UMASH.jointEvent seed x y
        (ProvenHashes.UMASH.keyPairsEquiv.symm
          (Function.update (Function.update (Function.update K ⟨i, ⋯⟩ abw.1.1) ⟨j, ⋯⟩ abw.1.2) 16 abw.2))) ≤
    1416246956032 / ↑ProvenHashes.UMASH.q ^ 2

ProvenHashes.UMASH.two_ph_compatibility_sum (S : Finset ℕ) (weight : ℕ → ℕ) (c : ℕ) :
  (∑ u ∈ S, ∑ v ∈ S, if (v ^^^ 2 * u) % 8 = c then weight v else 0) =
    ∑ r ∈ Finset.range 4, {u ∈ S | u % 4 = r}.card * ∑ v ∈ S with v % 8 = c ^^^ 2 * r, weight v

ProvenHashes.UMASH.two_ph_lane_compatibility (s t r : ℕ) (ht : 0 < t) (hst : t < s) (hr : r ≤ 64)
  (hrh : r ≤ ProvenHashes.UMASH.phDifferenceShift s t) (a b c d : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) :
  (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleLane s a ^^^ ProvenHashes.UMASH.phShuffleLane t b ^^^ d ^^^
          (a ^^^ b ^^^ c) <<< 1).toNat %

  (hr1 : 1 ≤ r) (u v : ℕ) (hu : 2 ^ r ∣ u) (hv : 2 ^ r ∣ v) :
  ProvenHashes.uniformProb (ProvenHashes.UMASH.lowRawTarget seed x y u v) ≤
    2 ^ r * ProvenHashes.UMASH.lowTargetK r (ProvenHashes.UMASH.phenhENHMask (x.chunks.length - (i + 1)) u v) /
      ↑ProvenHashes.UMASH.q ^ 2

ProvenHashes.UMASH.word_prefix_eq_iff_sub_dvd (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) (r : ℕ) (hr : r ≤ 64) :
  BitVec.toNat x % 2 ^ r = BitVec.toNat y % 2 ^ r ↔ 2 ^ r ∣ BitVec.toNat (y - x)
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.word_prefix_eq_iff_sub_dvd' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.wordValuation_eq_sub (x y : ProvenHashes.UMASH.Word) (hxy : x ≠ y) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.wordValuation x y = padicValNat 2 (BitVec.toNat (y - x))
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.wordValuation_eq_sub' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrapped_product_difference_bounds (Q δ ε A B : ℤ) (hQ : 0 < Q) (hA : 0 ≤ A ∧ A < Q)
  (hB : 0 ≤ B ∧ B < Q) :
  -Q ^ 2 < ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrappedProductDifferenceZ Q δ ε A B ∧
    ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrappedProductDifferenceZ Q δ ε A B < Q ^ 2

  (hδ : 0 ≤ δ ∧ δ < Q) (hε : 0 ≤ ε ∧ ε < Q) :
  ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrappedProductBranch Q δ ε (A, B) ∈ ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrappedProductBranches

ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrapped_product_branch_line (Q δ ε A B target i j k : ℤ)
  (hbranch : ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrappedProductBranch Q δ ε (A, B) = (i, j, k))
  (hres : ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrappedProductDifferenceZ Q δ ε A B ≡ target [ZMOD Q ^ 2]) :
  (δ - Q * i) * B + (ε - Q * j) * A = target % Q ^ 2 + Q ^ 2 * k - (δ - Q * i) * (ε - Q * j)

ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrapped_product_residue_interval_count (m : ℕ) (lo hi : Fin m → ℝ) (ell : ℝ) (Q δ ε target : ℤ)
  (S : Finset (ℤ × ℤ)) (hm : 0 < m) (hell : 0 < ell) (hQ : 0 < Q) (hδ : 0 < δ ∧ δ < Q) (hε : 0 < ε ∧ ε < Q)
  (hbox : ∀ z ∈ S, (0 ≤ z.1 ∧ z.1 < Q) ∧ 0 ≤ z.2 ∧ z.2 < Q)
  (hres : ∀ z ∈ S, ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrappedProductDifferenceZ Q δ ε z.1 z.2 ≡ target [ZMOD Q ^ 2])
  (hlen : ∀ (i : Fin m), lo i ≤ hi i ∧ hi i - lo i ≤ ell)
  (hdis : ∀ (i j : Fin m), i ≠ j → Disjoint (Set.Ico (lo i) (hi i)) (Set.Ico (lo j) (hi j)))
  (hcover : ∀ z ∈ S, ∃ i, lo i ≤ ↑(z.1 * z.2) ∧ ↑(z.1 * z.2) < hi i) : ↑S.card ≤ 8 * (2 * √(↑m * ell) + 2 * ↑m)
'ProvenHashes.UMASH.wrapped_product_residue_interval_count' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (hδ : 0 < δ ∧ δ < Q) (hε : 0 < ε ∧ ε < Q) (hbox : ∀ z ∈ S, (0 ≤ z.1 ∧ z.1 < Q) ∧ 0 ≤ z.2 ∧ z.2 < Q)

  (hhigh : ∀ z ∈ S, z.1 * z.2 / Q ∈ H) : ↑S.card ≤ 16 * (√(↑H.card * ↑Q) + ↑H.card)
